text-anchor="start" style="white-space: pre;" direction="ltr" dominant-baseline="alphabetic">R</text></g><g transform="translate(326.3824339990572 10) rotate(0 9.900001525878906 22.5)"><text x="0" y="31.5" font-family="Comic Shanns, monospace, Segoe UI Emoji" font-size="36px" fill="#1e1e1e" text-anchor="start" style="white-space: pre;" direction="ltr" dominant-baseline="alphabetic">C</text></g></svg>
$Z_{RC}=\frac{\frac{1}{j\omega C}·R}{\frac{1}{j\omega C}+R} \frac{/j\omega C}{/j\omega C}=\frac{R}{1+j\omega RC}$
$|R_{RC}|=\frac{|R|}{|1+j\omega RC|}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{ 1+\omega^{2} (RC)^{2}}}$

x="0" y="31.5" font-family="Comic Shanns, monospace, Segoe UI Emoji" font-size="36px" fill="#1e1e1e" text-anchor="start" style="white-space: pre;" direction="ltr" dominant-baseline="alphabetic">jwL</text></g></svg>

$Z_{LC}(j\omega)=j\omega L+\frac{1}{j\omega C}=j\omega L-j \frac{1}{j\omega C}=j\left( \omega L-\frac{1}{j\omega C} \right)$
$|Z_{LC}|=|j\left( \omega L-\frac{1}{\omega C} \right)|=|\omega L·\left( \frac{\omega C}{\omega C} \right)-\frac{1}{\omega C}|=|\frac{\omega^{2}LC-1}{\omega C}|=$
$=\frac{|\omega^{2}LC-1|}{\omega C}$

This will act as filter that blocks frequencies different than $f=\frac{1}{2\pi \sqrt{ LC }}$
Example filter with resonance frequency $50Hz$ to filter noise from AC line

---

$Z_{LC}=\frac{j\omega L· \frac{1}{j\omega C}}{j\omega L+ \frac{1}{j\omega C}}· \frac{j\omega C}{j\omega C}=\frac{j\omega L}{1-\omega^{2}LC}$
$|Z_{LC}|=\frac{|j\omega L|}{|1-\omega^{2}LC|}=\frac{\omega L}{|1-\omega^{2}LC|}$

$Z_{RLC}=R+j\omega L+ \frac{1}{j\omega C}=R+j\omega L-j \frac{1}{\omega C}=R+j\left( \omega L-\frac{1}{\omega C} \right)$
$|Z_{RLC}|=\sqrt{ R^{2}+\left( \omega L-\frac{1}{\omega C} \right)^{2} }=\sqrt{ R^{2}+\left( \frac{\omega^{2}LC-1}{\omega C} \right)^{2} }$

$Z_{RLC}= \frac{\frac{1}{j\omega C}·(R+J\omega L)}{\frac{1}{j\omega C}+R+J\omega L}· \frac{j\omega C}{j\omega C}=\frac{R+j\omega L}{1-\omega^{2}LC+j\omega RC}$
$|Z_{RLC}|=\frac{\sqrt{R^{2}+\omega^{2}L^{2}}}{\sqrt{ (1-\omega^{2}LC)^{2}+\omega^{2} }(RC)^{2}}$

$ku=\frac{U_{out}}{U_{in}}$
$U_{in}(j\omega),U_{out}(j\omega)\to ku(j\omega)\to |ku(j\omega)|$

---

RC low pass filter
$ku=\frac{U_{out}}{U_{in}}$
$U_{out}=U_{in} \frac{\frac{1}{j\omega c}}{\frac{1}{j\omega C}+R}· \frac{j\omega C}{j\omega C}$
$ku(j\omega)=\frac{1}{1+j\omega RC}$ 

$|ku|=\frac{1}{\sqrt{ 1+\omega^{2}(RC^{2}) }}$

$\varphi(A+jB)=\arctan \frac{B}{A}$
$\varphi\left( \frac{A}{B} \right)=\varphi(A)-\varphi(B)$

phase of RC low pass
$\varphi(\omega)=\varphi(1)-\varphi(1+j\omega RC)=0\degree-\arctan \frac{\omega RC}{1}$
$=-\arctan\omega RC$

$|ku|=\frac{1}{\sqrt{ 1+\omega^{2} (RC)^{2}}}$
$|ku|_{dB}=20\log \frac{1}{\sqrt{ 1+\omega^{2}(RC)^{2} }}=\overbrace{ 20\log 1 }^{ 0 }-20\log \sqrt{ 1+\omega^{2}(RC)^{2} }$

$\omega^{2}(RC)^{2}\ll 1$
$\omega\ll \frac{1}{RC}$
$-20\log \sqrt{ 1 }$
0dB

| $\omega^{2}(RC)^{2}\ll 1$ | $\omega^{2}(RC)^{2}\gg 1$      |
| ------------------------- | ------------------------------ |
| $\omega\ll \frac{1}{RC}$  | $\omega\gg \frac{1}{RC}$       |
| $-20\log \sqrt{ 1 }$      | $-20\log\omega(RC)$            |
| 0dB                       | $\omega=\frac{1}{RC}\to_{0}$   |
|                           | $\omega=\frac{10}{RC}\to -20$  |
|                           | $\omega=\frac{100}{RC}\to -40$ |
|                           | -20dB/dec                      |

$\omega=\frac{1}{RC}$
$=-20\sqrt{ 2 }\approx-3dB$

$ku(j\omega)=\frac{R}{R+\frac{1}{j\omega C}}=\frac{j\omega RC}{1+j\omega RC}$
$|ku|=\frac{|j\omega RC|}{|1+j\omega RC|}=\frac{\omega RC}{\sqrt{ 1+\omega^{2}(RC)^{2} }}$
$|ku|_{Db}=20\log |ku|=20\log \frac{\omega RC}{\sqrt{ 1+\omega^{2}(RC)^{2} }}$

$|ku|_{Db}=\overbrace{ 20\log\omega RC }^{ B }\overbrace{ -20\log \sqrt{ 1+\omega^{2}(RC)^{2} } }^{ A }$

Example this can be used to remove low frequencies going through speaker that can't be heard but the current going through will still generate heat in the speaker without filter

antenas that operate at M Hz don't care about low frequencies

RC low pass phase characteristic
$@\varphi(\omega)=arg(1)-arg(1-j\omega RC)=0\degree-\arctan\left( \frac{\omega RC}{1} \right)$

should be 1/RC not 0.1/RC
CR high pass phase characteristic
$\varphi(\omega)=\varphi(j\omega RC)-\arctan(\omega RC)=90\degree-\arctan(\omega RC)$

phase stabilizes after 1 dec

---

putting (10x) amplifiel after filter RC
offsets the graph (the graph starts from 20dB in this case instead of 0)

transform="translate(126.4999771118164 190) rotate(0 27.499961853027344 12.5)"><text x="0" y="17.5" font-family="Comic Shanns, monospace, Segoe UI Emoji" font-size="20px" fill="#1e1e1e" text-anchor="start" style="white-space: pre;" direction="ltr" dominant-baseline="alphabetic">1/jwC</text></g></svg>

$ku(j\omega)=\frac{\frac{1}{j\omega C}}{j\omega L+\frac{1}{j\omega C}}· \frac{j\omega C}{j\omega C}=\frac{1}{1-\omega^{2}LC}$
$|ku(\omega)|=\frac{1}{|1-\omega^{2}LC|}$
$|ku|_{dB}=\overbrace{ 20\log 1 }^{ 0 }-20\log |1-\omega^{2}LC|$

$-20\log |1-\omega^{2}LC|$


| $\omega^{2}LC<1$<br>            | $\omega^{2}LC>1$                |
| ------------------------------- | ------------------------------- |
| $\omega< \frac{1}{\sqrt{ LC }}$ | $\omega> \frac{1}{\sqrt{ LC }}$ |
| $-20\log \|1\|=0dB$             | $-20\log\omega^{2}LC$           |

assumption needed for something fun to happen
$\frac{1}{RC}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{ lC }}$


$ku=\frac{\frac{1}{j\omega C}+j\omega L}{R+\frac{1}{j\omega C}+j\omega L}· \frac{j\omega C}{j\omega C}=\frac{1-\omega^{2}lC}{1-\omega^{2}LC+j\omega RC}$
$|ku|=\frac{|1-\omega^{2}LC|}{\sqrt{ (1-\omega^{2}LC)^{2}+\omega^{2}(RC)^{2} }}$


| $\omega< \frac{1}{RC}$          | $\omega> \frac{1}{RC}$          |
| ------------------------------- | ------------------------------- |
| $\omega< \frac{1}{\sqrt{ LC }}$ | $\omega> \frac{1}{\sqrt{ LC }}$ |
| $\|ku\|=1$                      | $\|ku\|=1$                      |
| $(0db)$                         | $0(dB)$                         |
$|ku|=$
$\frac{\omega^{2}LC}{\sqrt{ \omega^{4}(LC)^{2}+\cancel{ \omega^{2}(RC)^{2} } }}=\frac{\omega^{2}LC}{\omega^{2}LC}=1$

${}@ \frac{1}{\sqrt{ LC }} \frac{1}{RC}=\frac{|1-1|}{\sqrt{ 0^{2}+1^{2} }}{}$
